  name: 'qf0-verdicts-entries-vc1',

const CONTEXT = `You are checking two draft RESULTS.md entries for a research repository. Repository: /Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab (git main, HEAD bfc179c, pushed). The draft: ${A.entry} (a scratch copy; the same text is in the working tree's RESULTS.md at lines 24-385, uncommitted, under "## Log — Phase 2", newest first). It holds two entries dated 2026-10-19: "PROPOSAL-m Q-F0 — VERDICTS" (first) and "PROPOSAL-m Q-F0 — secondary DVs" (second), for PROPOSAL-m v1.11 §8 step 17 (m:358).

HARD RULES (breaking any one fails your check):

const FIND = { type: 'object', properties: {
  checked: { type: 'string', description: 'what you read and ran, in brief' },
  findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, text: { type: 'string', description: 'the entry text checked, quoted' },
    verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['VERIFIED', 'WRONG', 'UNSUPPORTED', 'FORBIDDEN', 'IMPRECISE', 'MISSING'] },
    severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['fails', 'imprecise', 'note', 'none'] },
    evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'text', 'verdict', 'severity', 'evidence', 'correction'] } } },
  required: ['checked', 'findings'] }
const REF = { type: 'object', properties: {
  checked: { type: 'string' },
  rulings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, upheld: { type: 'boolean' }, rating: { type: 'string', enum: ['fails', 'imprecise', 'note', 'refuted'] },
    reason: { type: 'string' }, fix_ok: { type: 'string', description: 'is the proposed correction right? give the right one if not' } },
    required: ['id', 'upheld', 'rating', 'reason', 'fix_ok'] } },
  missed: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, text: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['fails', 'imprecise', 'note'] },
    evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['id', 'text', 'verdict', 'severity', 'evidence', 'correction'] } } },
  required: ['checked', 'rulings', 'missed'] }

const LENSES = [
  { key: 'fidelity', ask: `LENS: FIDELITY. Check every factual claim in both entries against its source: each figure, fraction, interval, hash (full and truncated forms), path, quotation, line number, commit id, section citation and attribution. Quotations character for character: the PI's words against pi_words.txt (the chat drops backticks; the block's own file has them); the seat's rulings and the block's text against the v1.1 file; the addendum's words against its file; the §5 sentence, the labels, the abstract clause, the §8.1 text and the dose note against Q-F0_VERDICTS.json; the secondary entry's quotes against Q-F0_secondary.json. Cover at least: the headline; "The words"; "The run" (preconditions vs raw §§1 and 4, the battery vs raw §2, the inputs, the refusal and diagnosis vs raw §§5-6, the second invocation vs raw §8, the outputs); "A0, with its §5 sentence" (m:326's order; (i)-(iii); every rate and every p with its test string; Δ_c's interval; the power; the guard); "The sham"; "The unit outcome"; "§7's v1 outputs" (licensing, labels, the v8 :176/:184 note against the preprint, ceiling, clause, §8.1, Step 7, the dose note, every enumeration row id with its clause and cells, the counts against v1.0 §2); "Counters by property"; "The per-property exclusion table"; "Beside"; "The release" (the gate picks and events, the seconds); "The looks" (the table and the printed side against the launch entry); the CSI item; "The seat's rulings"; "For the seat"; the links; and every figure of the secondary-DV entry. One finding per claim checked, VERIFIED ones included (severity none); ids F1, F2, ….` },
  { key: 'rules', ask: `LENS: RULES. Check both entries against what binds them: the seat's block v1.1 §§1-8 item by item (above all §5's list of the VERDICTS entry's contents: flag MISSING for any item it omits; §4's two commits and their order; §8, what comes back) and the addendum's items 1-4; the PI's words; the proposal's m:306-m:318, m:320, m:324-m:349 (Steps 1, 2, 5 and 7; §7.5) and m:358's step 17; the PIN readings the entries invoke (PIN-2, -3, -4, -13, -14, -17, -19, -21, -22, -25, -27, -32, -37, as scripts/m_analyze.py states them); lock v1.0 §§2-3. Flag (WRONG/IMPRECISE) any rule the entries misstate or misattribute (the seat's, the PI's, Code's and the proposal's words kept apart), any precedent or standing rule claimed that no committed record states, any reading of Code's not marked as Code's, any claim beyond what the VERDICTS output licenses (the secondaries issue no claim, m:358; Code's extractions are descriptive, m:320), and any departure from the block or the addendum that the entries do not disclose. Examine in particular: the "one invocation" reading (addendum item 2); Code's reading of "every path on the command line absolute"; the reads' order against m:318 ("performed after the analyzer's output exists and before the entry is committed") and the block's §§5-6; the count against v1.0 §2's 23 items; the labels note against the step-15b answers' "Two notes for the PI, not the table"; the dose note against §7.5 (c) and PIN-21; whether anything the entries call "Code's extraction" is presented as the analyzer's. Ids R1, R2, …; VERIFIED items too (severity none).` },
  { key: 'mechanics', ask: `LENS: MECHANICS. Recompute with tools, independently of the entries: every sha256 named (files on disk; committed blobs via git show bfc179c:path | shasum -a 256); every commit id (git cat-file -t); from the checkpoints (resume.read_checkpoint, then readrule.release_full): P 79/80, the per-map 40/41 and 39/39, the sham 18/32 and its 8/17 and 10/15, launched, domain and pre-pick counts per arm, the excluded sessions (A0 17, A0s 3), whole-session validity, at least six counter cells and the reads by position, the per-property table, the looks table (stages.campaign_look over the first n records), the seconds and each stage's hour_cap_ms_used; the gate stages' contrast picks and released events; the enumeration's groupings (from the output's v1_outputs.enumeration and the table); the secondary entry's (a) at positions 1-2, (b) both rates, (d) A0's per-pair and habit figures, (e); p_up = 81/2^80 and its decimal; 1 - dead_tail; the power figures. Check every line anchor (scripts/m_analyze.py:1768-1773, :8018, :1929-1931, :1645-1677, :8224-8232; valence_core/m/stages.py:1937, :1973, :2390; valence_core/m/readrule.py:799; scripts/m_sessions.py:529 and :1286) and every raw section or line cited (§§1-8, 6.3, 6.3b, 7, 8.1, 8.2, line 499; extractions E1-E9). Check the links' paths (the values-check raw does not exist yet: note it, do not flag it). Ids M1, M2, …; VERIFIED items too (severity none).` },
]

const results = await pipeline(LENSES,
  l => agent(`${CONTEXT}\n\n${l.ask}\n\nBe exhaustive and exact; a claim you could not check is UNSUPPORTED with the reason.`, { label: `check:${l.key}`, phase: 'Check', schema: FIND }),
  (rep, l) => {
    const open = (rep && rep.findings || []).filter(f => f.verdict !== 'VERIFIED')
    return agent(`${CONTEXT}\n\nYou are a STRICT REFUTER for the ${l.key} lens of this values-check. Its findings that are not VERIFIED follow as JSON. For each, try to refute it from the sources: uphold it only if it is clearly right; rate it fails (the entry states something false, or omits a required item), imprecise, note, or refuted; judge its correction (fix_ok). Then look for what the lens missed in its own scope (${l.key}) and return those in missed[] (ids X1, X2, …). The lens also marked ${(rep && rep.findings || []).length - open.length} claims VERIFIED; spot-check at least five of them and put any you overturn in missed[].\n\nThe lens's open findings:\n${JSON.stringify(open, null, 1)}\n\nThe lens's VERIFIED claims (ids and texts):\n${JSON.stringify((rep && rep.findings || []).filter(f => f.verdict === 'VERIFIED').map(f => ({ id: f.id, text: f.text })), null, 1)}`,
      { label: `refute:${l.key}`, phase: 'Refute', schema: REF }).then(ref => ({ lens: l.key, report: rep, refute: ref }))
  })
return results

---- apply_vc1.py, verbatim
"""Pass-1 dispositions applied to the two draft entries (whitespace-insensitive, one match each, asserted)."""
import re, sys

def rep(s, old, new, label):
    pat = r"\s+".join(re.escape(w) for w in old.split())
    m = list(re.finditer(pat, s))
    assert len(m) == 1, (label, len(m))
    return s[:m[0].start()] + new + s[m[0].end():]

V, SEC = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
v = open(V, encoding="utf-8").read()

"""Code's checks of the figures the pass-1 dispositions add to the entries (read-only, model-free)."""
import json, sys
from pathlib import Path
REPO = Path(sys.argv[1]); sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO))
from valence_core.m import resume, readrule
V = json.loads((REPO / "data/m/verdicts/Q-F0_VERDICTS.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
T = json.loads((REPO / "data/m/gates/m_posting_sentences.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
rec = {s: readrule.release_full(resume.read_checkpoint(REPO / f"data/m/ckpt/Q-F0_{s}.json")["sessions"]) for s in ("campaign", "time", "qual")}
c = [r for r in rec["campaign"] if r["template"] == "A0"]
later = [ch for r in c for ch in (r["choices"] or [])[1:]]
print("V1 A0 reads after the first:", len(later), "low label mass", sum(bool(x["low_label_mass"]) for x in later),
      "top1_not_label", sum(bool(x["top1_not_label"]) for x in later), "| all reads", sum(len(r["choices"] or []) for r in c),
      "| reads in non-domain sessions", sum(len(r["choices"] or []) for r in c if not r["domain"]))
rows = {r["id"]: r for r in T["rows"]}
r = rows["R013.11"]
print("V2 R013.11 kinds", r["kinds"], "null_cells", r["null_cells"], "loc", r["loc"], "| text:", r["text"][-160:])
print("   rows with kind abstract-olmo-clause:", [x["id"] for x in T["rows"] if "abstract-olmo-clause" in x["kinds"]])
print("   R013.11 in the enumeration:", [(e["row_id"], e["clauses"], [x["cell"] for x in e["a"]["cells"]]) for e in V["v1_outputs"]["enumeration"] if e["row_id"] == "R013.11"])
print("V3 rows with S1 among null_cells:", [(x["id"], x["null_cells"]) for x in T["rows"] if "S1" in x["null_cells"]])
for stg in ("time", "qual"):
    a0 = [r for r in rec[stg] if r["template"] == "A0"]
    picks = [(r["s"], [ch["picked_role"] for ch in r["choices"]]) for r in a0]
    n = sum(len(p) for _, p in picks); nP = sum(x == "P" for _, p in picks for x in p)
    print(f"V4 {stg}: A0 picks per session {picks}; total {n}, on P {nP}; later picks (after the first) {n - len(a0)}")
    adv = [(r["s"], x.get("ci"), x["property"], (x["audit"] or {}).get("claim"), (x["audit"] or {}).get("advisory"))
           for r in a0 for x in r["rounds"] if x["phase"] == "free" and (x["audit"] or {}).get("advisory")]
    print(f"V5 {stg}: A0 free rounds with an advisory: {adv}")
    fr = [x for r in a0 for x in r["rounds"] if x["phase"] == "free"]
    print(f"   {stg}: A0 free rounds {len(fr)} by property {sorted(set(x['property'] for x in fr))}; counters_beside free_rounds "
          f"{V['gate_release'][stg]['counters_beside']['denominators']['free_rounds']}")
print("V6 PIN-27 text:")
src = (REPO / "scripts/m_analyze.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
for i in (1369, 1370, 1371, 2893, 2894):
    print(f"   m_analyze.py:{i}: {src[i-1].strip()[:200]}")
for i in (1173, 1174, 1252, 1253, 1254, 1255, 1256, 8181, 1986):

  checked: { type: 'string', description: 'what you read and ran, in brief' },
  findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, text: { type: 'string', description: 'the entry text checked, quoted' },
    verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['VERIFIED', 'WRONG', 'UNSUPPORTED', 'FORBIDDEN', 'IMPRECISE', 'MISSING'] },
    severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['fails', 'imprecise', 'note', 'none'] },
    evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'text', 'verdict', 'severity', 'evidence', 'correction'] } } },
  required: ['checked', 'findings'] }
const REF = { type: 'object', properties: {
  checked: { type: 'string' },
  rulings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, upheld: { type: 'boolean' }, rating: { type: 'string', enum: ['fails', 'imprecise', 'note', 'refuted'] },
    reason: { type: 'string' }, fix_ok: { type: 'string', description: 'is the proposed correction right? give the right one if not' } },
    required: ['id', 'upheld', 'rating', 'reason', 'fix_ok'] } },
  missed: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, text: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['fails', 'imprecise', 'note'] },
    evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['id', 'text', 'verdict', 'severity', 'evidence', 'correction'] } } },
  required: ['checked', 'rulings', 'missed'] }

const LENSES = [
  { key: 'fidelity', ask: `LENS: FIDELITY. First, every pass-1 fix (diff the draft against entries.pre-vc1.md): is each changed passage exact against its source? Then every factual claim in both entries against its source again, with priority to text pass 1 marked VERIFIED only by inspection: each figure, fraction, interval, hash (full and truncated), path, quotation (character for character: the PI's words against pi_words.txt; the seat's text against the v1.1 and addendum files; the output's strings against Q-F0_VERDICTS.json and Q-F0_secondary.json), line number, commit id, section citation and attribution, in both entries. One finding per claim checked, VERIFIED ones included (severity none); ids F1, F2, ….` },
  { key: 'rules', ask: `LENS: RULES. First, every pass-1 fix that touches a rule, an attribution or a reading (R4, R7, R8, R13, R17, R20, R24, R28, R33, X1, X2 and fidelity X1): is it right now, and complete? Then, afresh: both entries against the seat's block v1.1 §§1-8 item by item (§5's list: MISSING for any omitted item; §4's commits; §8) and the addendum's items 1-4; the PI's words; m:306-m:320, m:324-m:349 and m:358's step 17; the PIN readings invoked (as scripts/m_analyze.py states them); lock v1.0 §§2-3. Flag misstated or misattributed rules (the seat's, the PI's, Code's and the proposal's words kept apart), any precedent or standing rule claimed that no committed record states, Code's readings not marked as Code's, claims beyond what the output licenses, undisclosed departures, and any new sentence the fixes introduced that overstates (e.g. "every", "all", "none", "each") without a source. Ids R1, R2, …; VERIFIED items too (severity none).` },
  { key: 'mechanics', ask: `LENS: MECHANICS. First, recompute every figure the pass-1 fixes added (the reads after the first, 390/24/49; the 46 gate-stage picks and their roles; s = 5's two advisories at choice indices 2 and 5; R180.02 and R180.03; R013.11's kinds; the 14 + 16 + 2 count; the campaign raw's header line 13; line anchors m_analyze.py:1173, :1256-1261, :1986, :8181, :8224-8232; the extractions raw's E10 and E11 and its 17/17). Then re-run, independently, a fresh sample of the rest: at least ten figures across A0, the sham, the counters, the per-property table, the looks table, the seconds and the secondary entry, from the checkpoints and the outputs; every sha256 and commit id named; every raw section cited (§§1-8, 2.2, 6.3, 6.3b, 7, 8.1, 8.2, line 499; E1-E11). Check the links' paths (the values-check raw does not exist yet: note it, do not flag it). Ids M1, M2, …; VERIFIED items too (severity none).` },
]

const results = await pipeline(LENSES,
  l => agent(`${CONTEXT}\n\n${l.ask}\n\nBe exhaustive and exact; a claim you could not check is UNSUPPORTED with the reason.`, { label: `check:${l.key}`, phase: 'Check', schema: FIND }),
  (rep, l) => {
    const open = (rep && rep.findings || []).filter(f => f.verdict !== 'VERIFIED')
    return agent(`${CONTEXT}\n\nYou are a STRICT REFUTER for the ${l.key} lens of this values-check's pass 2. Its findings that are not VERIFIED follow as JSON. For each, try to refute it from the sources: uphold it only if it is clearly right; rate it fails (the entry states something false, or omits a required item), imprecise, note, or refuted; judge its correction (fix_ok). Then look for what the lens missed in its own scope (${l.key}), including any pass-1 fix that is wrong or incomplete, and return those in missed[] (ids X1, X2, …). The lens also marked ${(rep && rep.findings || []).length - open.length} claims VERIFIED; spot-check at least five of them and put any you overturn in missed[].\n\nThe lens's open findings:\n${JSON.stringify(open, null, 1)}\n\nThe lens's VERIFIED claims (ids and texts):\n${JSON.stringify((rep && rep.findings || []).filter(f => f.verdict === 'VERIFIED').map(f => ({ id: f.id, text: f.text })), null, 1)}`,
      { label: `refute:${l.key}`, phase: 'Refute', schema: REF }).then(ref => ({ lens: l.key, report: rep, refute: ref }))
  })
return results

---- apply_vc2.py, verbatim
"""Pass-2 dispositions applied to the two draft entries (whitespace-insensitive, one match each, asserted)."""
import re, sys

def rep(s, old, new, label):
    pat = r"\s+".join(re.escape(w) for w in old.split())
    m = list(re.finditer(pat, s))
    assert len(m) == 1, (label, len(m))
    return s[:m[0].start()] + new + s[m[0].end():]

V, SEC = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
v = open(V, encoding="utf-8").read()
